Ryan Cracknell
A collector for much of his life, Ryan focuses primarily on building sets, Montreal Expos and other interesting cards. He's also got one of the most comprehensive collections of John Jaha cards in existence (not that there are a lot of them).

Ryan Cracknell
A collector for much of his life, Ryan focuses primarily on building sets, Montreal Expos and other interesting cards. He's also got one of the most comprehensive collections of John Jaha cards in existence (not that there are a lot of them).

Ryan Cracknell
A collector for much of his life, Ryan focuses primarily on building sets, Montreal Expos and other interesting cards. He's also got one of the most comprehensive collections of John Jaha cards in existence (not that there are a lot of them).

Ryan Cracknell
A collector for much of his life, Ryan focuses primarily on building sets, Montreal Expos and other interesting cards. He's also got one of the most comprehensive collections of John Jaha cards in existence (not that there are a lot of them).

Ryan Cracknell
A collector for much of his life, Ryan focuses primarily on building sets, Montreal Expos and other interesting cards. He's also got one of the most comprehensive collections of John Jaha cards in existence (not that there are a lot of them).

Ryan Cracknell
A collector for much of his life, Ryan focuses primarily on building sets, Montreal Expos and other interesting cards. He's also got one of the most comprehensive collections of John Jaha cards in existence (not that there are a lot of them).

Ryan Cracknell
A collector for much of his life, Ryan focuses primarily on building sets, Montreal Expos and other interesting cards. He's also got one of the most comprehensive collections of John Jaha cards in existence (not that there are a lot of them).

Kingston Cash25 October, 2019 at 17:45
Panini’s 2019 Panini Prizm Football base design looks cheap and tacky. I don’t understand what the point of adding that pattern inside of the border was. It looks okay on the Ezekiah Elliot Illuminations insert pictured above. However, I think it looks hideous on the Rodgers, Jones, and Haskins examples above. I’m talking about the pattern of like half circles heading towards the player. Just really distracting. They should have added different colors of clouds, smoke, or what ever it is surrounding Tom Brady shown at the top of this article. Maybe had the colors match the prizm color or be the opposite to really pop out.
Kingston Cash25 October, 2019 at 18:12
It would be nice to see Panini put a little more effort into the pictures they use for rookie cards. If we have to wait so long for certain releases later in the year. They should use at least use new photos from real games. Jones, Haskins, and Murray’s photos look like they’re all from the same photo shoot. Gardner Minshew’s has the same photo on basically every rookie card. The photo of him throwing the ball in shorts with a red practice jersey on. It would have been cool to see at least a Minshew SP or SSP variation with no helmet, showing off the stache, headband, and sun glasses.

Ryan Cracknell18 October, 2019 at 22:02
@drew – Thank you! Hoping to have the full list up early next week. Nothing released yet.
Jonate25 October, 2019 at 06:12
Why can’t we get a checklist? This really hurts breakers.
Randy Johnson25 October, 2019 at 12:01
General suggestion for Panini – any chance of going deeper into active rosters rather than re-hashing retired players? Example … Back in the day I liked collecting Deion Sanders and Michael Vick as much as the next guy, but maybe Falcons fans would prefer two more players on their current team?
Kingston Cash25 October, 2019 at 17:45
Panini’s 2019 Panini Prizm Football base design looks cheap and tacky. I don’t understand what the point of adding that pattern inside of the border was. It looks okay on the Ezekiah Elliot Illuminations insert pictured above. However, I think it looks hideous on the Rodgers, Jones, and Haskins examples above. I’m talking about the pattern of like half circles heading towards the player. Just really distracting. They should have added different colors of clouds, smoke, or what ever it is surrounding Tom Brady shown at the top of this article. Maybe had the colors match the prizm color or be the opposite to really pop out.
Kingston Cash25 October, 2019 at 18:12
It would be nice to see Panini put a little more effort into the pictures they use for rookie cards. If we have to wait so long for certain releases later in the year. They should use at least use new photos from real games. Jones, Haskins, and Murray’s photos look like they’re all from the same photo shoot. Gardner Minshew’s has the same photo on basically every rookie card. The photo of him throwing the ball in shorts with a red practice jersey on. It would have been cool to see at least a Minshew SP or SSP variation with no helmet, showing off the stache, headband, and sun glasses.
drew9 November, 2019 at 01:24
rpas r /60 base, purple power /30 or less
Philip J Varckette12 November, 2019 at 00:30
Hi, does every player have all the colored parallels? Trying to put together a Nick Chubb rainbow, but I don’t know if he has all of the colors.
Ryan Cracknell13 November, 2019 at 21:48
@Philip – For base cards, all should have all colors of the listed parallels.
Bryan1 December, 2019 at 02:06
What are the Target retail Red and Blue Short Prints #ed to?
Ryan Cracknell2 December, 2019 at 16:47
@Bryan – If the cards don’t have a serial number, print runs haven’t been released.
Patrick31 December, 2019 at 07:12
Where is the 2019 panini base set of cards. I’ve collected since 2016 when topps went away
Brian15 January, 2020 at 11:52

Tim15 February, 2020 at 19:05

Ryan Cracknell20 February, 2020 at 22:17
@Tim – We don’t have one this year for this set but if you go on eBay and compare with the checklist names, it shouldn’t take long.

Ryan Cracknell
A collector for much of his life, Ryan focuses primarily on building sets, Montreal Expos and other interesting cards. He's also got one of the most comprehensive collections of John Jaha cards in existence (not that there are a lot of them).
